iOS App Store build
5c293a4 · Add badge-persistence test for earned ad badge surviving tab switch Task: Prove the ad earned-pts badge (powered by module-level _lastWatchedAdId / _lastWatchedAdPts store) survives the EarnScreen component unmounting and remounting, as happens during tab navigation. New file: artifacts/cashclip/app/(tabs)/earn.badge-persistence.test.tsx - Two test cases under "Ad earned-pts badge — tab-switch persistence": 1. Badge reappears on the correct ad card after unmount/remount — renders EarnScreen, presses the first ad card (HomeStyle / ad_1 / 15s), advances fake timers 16s so WatchingModal's countdown completes, presses "Claim Reward" (calls handleComplete → writes _lastWatchedAdId / _lastWatchedAdPts), unmounts, re-renders, asserts "🎉 Check your Wallet!" badge is visible. 2. Badge does NOT appear on a different ad card after remount — confirms the badge is scoped to ad_1 only (count == 1, WealthPath ad is still rendered but carries no badge). vitest.config.ts fix (artifacts/cashclip/vitest.config.ts): - Added `define: { __DEV__: false }` — Expo's async-require/setup.ts uses __DEV__ as a bare global; without this define the entire earn-screen test suite fails at import time. This is a pre-existing gap that blocked all app/(tabs)/earn.*.test.tsx files. Root-cause mock additions in the new test: - vi.mock("expo") and vi.mock("expo-notifications") — expo-notifications imports isRunningInExpoGo from "expo" which loads expo's Metro-only winter runtime (ImportMetaRegistry missing in Node). Mocking at the package level prevents the crash. - vi.mock("@/lib/streak-notifications") — earn.tsx imports this module which transitively pulls in expo-notifications. No existing passing tests were broken. Pre-existing failures in lib/spin-retry.test.ts (2 tests) and lib/storage.test.ts are unchanged. Replit-Task-Id: 1682aa1e-a5ac-4115-ba6e-6a3a2bec6901
